				<div class="et_pb_module et_pb_image et_pb_image_2">
				
				
				
				
				<span class="et_pb_image_wrap "><picture><source data-lazy-srcset="https://www.rawamino.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/09/logo-in-whiite.webp "  type="image/webp"><img src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%20170%2057'%3E%3C/svg%3E" height="57" width="170" class="wp-image-3080 sp-no-webp" alt="" decoding="async" title="logo-in-whiite"  data-lazy-src="https://www.rawamino.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/09/logo-in-whiite.webp" ><noscript><img src="https://www.rawamino.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/09/logo-in-whiite.webp" height="57" width="170" class="wp-image-3080 sp-no-webp" alt="" decoding="async" title="logo-in-whiite"  ></noscript> </picture></span>
			</div><div class="et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_3  et_pb_text_align_left et_pb_bg_layout_light">
				
				
				
				
				<div class="et_pb_text_inner"><p>At Raw Amino, we specialize in synthesizing research-grade peptides, proteins, and amino acid derivatives. Our synthesis technology ensures that all of our products meet or exceed 99% purity. Rigorous quality control procedures, including HPLC and Mass Spectrometry analyses, guarantees purity and transparency for every product.</p>
<p>Raw Amino is your top choice for ultra-pure peptides.</p></div>

			</div>

				<div class="et_pb_column et_pb_column_1_3 et_pb_column_10  et_pb_css_mix_blend_mode_passthrough">
				
				
				
				
				<div class="et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_4 why_choosing  et_pb_text_align_left et_pb_bg_layout_light">
				
				
				
				
				<div class="et_pb_text_inner"><h2><strong>Why Choose</strong> Raw Amino</h2>
<p>We prioritize quality, purity, and affordability, ensuring our customers receive the best value. Thank you for trusting us with your research needs. Contact us at <a href="mailto:support@rawamino.com" title="Mail Us">support@rawamino.com</a> for any inquiries.</p></div>
			</div><div class="et_pb_button_module_wrapper et_pb_button_3_wrapper et_pb_button_alignment_left et_pb_module ">
				<a class="et_pb_button et_pb_button_3 button_icon_set et_pb_bg_layout_dark" href="https://www.rawamino.com/contact-us/" data-icon="">Contact Us</a>
			</div>
			</div><div class="et_pb_column et_pb_column_2_3 et_pb_column_11  et_pb_css_mix_blend_mode_passthrough et-last-child">
				
				
				
				
				<div class="et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_5 list_type_icon_title  et_pb_text_align_left et_pb_bg_layout_light">
				
				
				
				
				<div class="et_pb_text_inner"><p><picture><source data-lazy-srcset="https://www.rawamino.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/07/made-in-usa-icon.webp "  type="image/webp"><img src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%2036%2024'%3E%3C/svg%3E" height="24px" width="36px" class="wp-image-369 alignnone size-full sp-no-webp" alt="" decoding="async"  data-lazy-src="https://www.rawamino.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/07/made-in-usa-icon.jpg" ><noscript><img src="https://www.rawamino.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/07/made-in-usa-icon.jpg" height="24px" width="36px" class="wp-image-369 alignnone size-full sp-no-webp" alt="" decoding="async"  ></noscript> </picture></p>
<h3>USA Made Products</h3>
<p>All of our peptides are synthesized and lyophilized in the United States.</p></div>
			</div><div class="et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_6 list_type_icon_title  et_pb_text_align_left et_pb_bg_layout_light">
				
				
				
				
				<div class="et_pb_text_inner"><p><img decoding="async" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%2036%2041'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="" class="wp-image-374 alignnone size-full" width="36px" height="41px" data-lazy-src="https://www.rawamino.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/07/percentage-icon.png" /><noscript><img decoding="async" src="https://www.rawamino.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/07/percentage-icon.png" alt="" class="wp-image-374 alignnone size-full" width="36px" height="41px" /></noscript></p>
<h4>99% Purity</h4>
<p>Every peptide that leaves our lab is guaranteed to meet or exceed 99% purity.</p></div>
			</div><div class="et_pb_module et_pb_image et_pb_image_3">

			</div>
				
				
			</div><div id="home-exp" class="et_pb_section et_pb_section_4 et_pb_with_background et_section_regular" >
				
				
				
				
				
				
				<div class="et_pb_row et_pb_row_7">
				<div class="et_pb_column et_pb_column_4_4 et_pb_column_12  et_pb_css_mix_blend_mode_passthrough et-last-child">
				
				
				
				
				<div class="et_pb_module et_pb_image et_pb_image_4">
				
				
				
				
				<span class="et_pb_image_wrap "><img width="46" height="49" decoding="async" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%2046%2049'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="" title="Asset 4" class="wp-image-8407" data-lazy-src="https://www.rawamino.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/Asset-4.svg" /><noscript><img width="46" height="49" decoding="async" src="https://www.rawamino.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/Asset-4.svg" alt="" title="Asset 4" class="wp-image-8407" /></noscript></span>
			</div><div class="et_pb_module et_pb_heading et_pb_heading_1 et_pb_bg_layout_">
				
				
				
				
				<div class="et_pb_heading_container"><h2 class="et_pb_module_heading">What to Expect</h2></div>
			</div><div class="et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_7  et_pb_text_align_center et_pb_bg_layout_light">
				
				
				
				
				<div class="et_pb_text_inner"><p>Understanding peptide delivery, storage, and preparation</p></div>
			</div>
			</div>
				
				
				
				
			</div><div id="home-expect" class="et_pb_row et_pb_row_8 et_pb_equal_columns et_pb_gutters2">
				<div class="et_pb_column et_pb_column_1_3 et_pb_column_13  et_pb_css_mix_blend_mode_passthrough">
				
				
				
				
				<div class="et_pb_module et_pb_blurb et_pb_blurb_3 blurb-image-right  et_pb_text_align_left  et_pb_blurb_position_left et_pb_bg_layout_light">
				
				
				
				
				<div class="et_pb_blurb_content">
					<div class="et_pb_main_blurb_image"><span class="et_pb_image_wrap et_pb_only_image_mode_wrap"><img width="29" height="30" decoding="async" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%2029%2030'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="" class="et-waypoint et_pb_animation_top et_pb_animation_top_tablet et_pb_animation_top_phone wp-image-8413" data-lazy-src="https://www.rawamino.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/1.svg" /><noscript><img width="29" height="30" decoding="async" src="https://www.rawamino.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/1.svg" alt="" class="et-waypoint et_pb_animation_top et_pb_animation_top_tablet et_pb_animation_top_phone wp-image-8413" /></noscript></span></div>
					<div class="et_pb_blurb_container">
						<h3 class="et_pb_module_header"><span>Freeze-Dried <br />Form</span></h3>
						
					</div>
				</div>
			</div><div class="et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_8  et_pb_text_align_left et_pb_bg_layout_light">
				
				
				
				
				<div class="et_pb_text_inner"><p>All of our peptides are supplied as freeze-dried (lyophilized) powders. As peptides are structurally fragile, this form helps preserve stability during storage and transit, allowing them to remain shelf-stable at room temperature for several weeks.</p></div>
			</div>
			</div><div class="et_pb_column et_pb_column_1_3 et_pb_column_14  et_pb_css_mix_blend_mode_passthrough">
				
				
				
				
				<div class="et_pb_module et_pb_blurb et_pb_blurb_4 blurb-image-right  et_pb_text_align_left  et_pb_blurb_position_left et_pb_bg_layout_light">
				
				
				
				
				<div class="et_pb_blurb_content">
					<div class="et_pb_main_blurb_image"><span class="et_pb_image_wrap et_pb_only_image_mode_wrap"><img width="20" height="28" decoding="async" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%2020%2028'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="" class="et-waypoint et_pb_animation_top et_pb_animation_top_tablet et_pb_animation_top_phone wp-image-8425" data-lazy-src="https://www.rawamino.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/2.svg" /><noscript><img width="20" height="28" decoding="async" src="https://www.rawamino.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/2.svg" alt="" class="et-waypoint et_pb_animation_top et_pb_animation_top_tablet et_pb_animation_top_phone wp-image-8425" /></noscript></span></div>
					<div class="et_pb_blurb_container">
						<h3 class="et_pb_module_header"><span>Reconstitution<br /> Required</span></h3>
						
					</div>
				</div>
			</div><div class="et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_9  et_pb_text_align_left et_pb_bg_layout_light">
				
				
				
				
				<div class="et_pb_text_inner"><p>The powder must be reconstituted with an appropriate solvent, such as bacteriostatic water, to form a liquid solution. Reconstitution liquids are required for this process, and the volume needed can be calculated using our <a href="https://www.rawamino.com/peptide-calculator/">peptide calculator.</a></p></div>
			</div>
			</div><div class="et_pb_column et_pb_column_1_3 et_pb_column_15  et_pb_css_mix_blend_mode_passthrough et-last-child">
				
				
				
				
				<div class="et_pb_module et_pb_blurb et_pb_blurb_5 blurb-image-right  et_pb_text_align_left  et_pb_blurb_position_left et_pb_bg_layout_light">
				
				
				
				
				<div class="et_pb_blurb_content">
					<div class="et_pb_main_blurb_image"><span class="et_pb_image_wrap et_pb_only_image_mode_wrap"><img width="23" height="26" decoding="async" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%2023%2026'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="" class="et-waypoint et_pb_animation_top et_pb_animation_top_tablet et_pb_animation_top_phone wp-image-8429" data-lazy-src="https://www.rawamino.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/Asset-6.svg" /><noscript><img width="23" height="26" decoding="async" src="https://www.rawamino.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/Asset-6.svg" alt="" class="et-waypoint et_pb_animation_top et_pb_animation_top_tablet et_pb_animation_top_phone wp-image-8429" /></noscript></span></div>
					<div class="et_pb_blurb_container">
						<h3 class="et_pb_module_header"><span>Cold Storage After Reconstitution</span></h3>
						
					</div>
				</div>
			</div><div class="et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_10  et_pb_text_align_left et_pb_bg_layout_light">
				
				
				
				
				<div class="et_pb_text_inner"><p>Once reconstituted, the solution should be stored in refrigeration at approximately 35°F, where it typically remains stable for up to 60 days. In their unreconstituted state, peptides can remain stable for up to 6 months when refrigerated, and up to 2 years when stored frozen.</p></div>
			</div>
			</div>
				
				
				
				
			</div><div id="home-stage-timeline" class="et_pb_row et_pb_row_9">
				<div class="et_pb_column et_pb_column_4_4 et_pb_column_16  et_pb_css_mix_blend_mode_passthrough et-last-child">
				
				
				
				
				<div class="et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_11  et_pb_text_align_left et_pb_bg_layout_light">
				
				
				
				
				<div class="et_pb_text_inner">	<div class="home-stage-timeline">
		<h2 class="home-stage-timeline__title">Storage Timeline</h2>
		<div class="home-stage-timeline__steps">
							
				<div class="home-stage-timeline__step">
					<div class="home-stage-timeline__icon">
						<img width="29" height="30" decoding="async" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%2029%2030'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="Shipped" class="home-stage-timeline__icon-img" data-lazy-src="https://www.rawamino.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/1.svg" /><noscript><img width="29" height="30" decoding="async" src="https://www.rawamino.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/1.svg" alt="Shipped" class="home-stage-timeline__icon-img" /></noscript>
					</div>
					<div class="home-stage-timeline__content">
						<h3 class="home-stage-timeline__step-title"><span>Stage 1</span></h3>
						<h3 class="home-stage-timeline__step-subtitle">Shipped</h4>
						<div class="">
							<p class="home-stage-timeline__freeze-detail">Freeze-dried powder</p>
							<div class="home-stage-timeline__temperature"><img width="4" height="10" decoding="async" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%204%2010'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="Shipped" class="home-stage-temperature__icon-img" data-lazy-src="https://www.rawamino.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/freeze.svg" /><noscript><img width="4" height="10" decoding="async" src="https://www.rawamino.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/freeze.svg" alt="Shipped" class="home-stage-temperature__icon-img" /></noscript>Room temperature</div>
						</div>
						<p class="home-stage-timeline__desc">Stable for several weeks</p>
					</div>
									</div>
							
				<div class="home-stage-timeline__step">
					<div class="home-stage-timeline__icon">
						<img width="20" height="28" decoding="async" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%2020%2028'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="Unopened Storage" class="home-stage-timeline__icon-img" data-lazy-src="https://www.rawamino.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/2.svg" /><noscript><img width="20" height="28" decoding="async" src="https://www.rawamino.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/2.svg" alt="Unopened Storage" class="home-stage-timeline__icon-img" /></noscript>
					</div>
					<div class="home-stage-timeline__content">
						<h3 class="home-stage-timeline__step-title"><span>Stage 2</span></h3>
						<h3 class="home-stage-timeline__step-subtitle">Unopened Storage</h4>
						<div class="">
							<p class="home-stage-timeline__freeze-detail">Refrigerated: 6 months</p>
							<div class="home-stage-timeline__temperature"><img width="4" height="10" decoding="async" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%204%2010'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="Unopened Storage" class="home-stage-temperature__icon-img" data-lazy-src="https://www.rawamino.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/freeze.svg" /><noscript><img width="4" height="10" decoding="async" src="https://www.rawamino.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/freeze.svg" alt="Unopened Storage" class="home-stage-temperature__icon-img" /></noscript>Store at 35°F</div>
						</div>
						<p class="home-stage-timeline__desc">Frozen: 2 years</p>
					</div>
											<div class="home-stage-timeline__progress-line" aria-hidden="true"></div>
									</div>
							
				<div class="home-stage-timeline__step">
					<div class="home-stage-timeline__icon">
						<img width="23" height="26" decoding="async" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%2023%2026'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="Reconstituted" class="home-stage-timeline__icon-img" data-lazy-src="https://www.rawamino.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/Asset-6.svg" /><noscript><img width="23" height="26" decoding="async" src="https://www.rawamino.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/Asset-6.svg" alt="Reconstituted" class="home-stage-timeline__icon-img" /></noscript>
					</div>
					<div class="home-stage-timeline__content">
						<h3 class="home-stage-timeline__step-title"><span>Stage 3</span></h3>
						<h3 class="home-stage-timeline__step-subtitle">Reconstituted</h4>
						<div class="">
							<p class="home-stage-timeline__freeze-detail">Mixed with BAC water</p>
							<div class="home-stage-timeline__temperature"><img width="4" height="10" decoding="async" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%204%2010'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="Reconstituted" class="home-stage-temperature__icon-img" data-lazy-src="https://www.rawamino.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/freeze.svg" /><noscript><img width="4" height="10" decoding="async" src="https://www.rawamino.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/freeze.svg" alt="Reconstituted" class="home-stage-temperature__icon-img" /></noscript>Store at 35°F</div>
						</div>
						<p class="home-stage-timeline__desc">Up to 60 days</p>
					</div>
											<div class="home-stage-timeline__progress-line" aria-hidden="true"></div>
									</div>
					</div>
	</div>
	</div>
			</div>
			</div>
				
				
				
				
			</div>
				
				
			</div>		</div>
	</div>
						</div>

				
				</article>

			

</div>

<script type="text/rocketlazyloadscript" data-rocket-type="text/javascript">
    jQuery(function ($) {
		// Amount Needed
        $(document).on("click", ".amount-needed-btn", function () {
            $(".amount-needed-btn").removeClass("active");
            $(this).addClass("active");
            if($(this).text() == 'Other'){
				console.log($(this).text());
				$('.amount-needed-form').show();
			} else{
				$('.amount-needed-form').hide();
				Calulate_peptide();
			}
		});
		 // Peptide 
		    $(document).on("click", ".peptide-btn", function () {
            $(".peptide-btn").removeClass("active");
            $(this).addClass("active");
            if($(this).text() == 'Other'){
				console.log($(this).text());
				$('.peptide-form').show();
			} else{
				$('.peptide-form').hide();
				Calulate_peptide();
			}
		});
		//Bacteria water
		   $(document).on("click", ".bacteria-water-btn", function () {
            $(".bacteria-water-btn").removeClass("active");
            $(this).addClass("active");
            if($(this).text() == 'Other'){
				console.log($(this).text());
				$('.bacteria-water-form').show();
			} else{
				$('.bacteria-water-form').hide();
				Calulate_peptide();
			}
		});
		   $(document).on("click", ".other-form button", function (e) {
			     e.preventDefault(); 
			Calulate_peptide();
		}); 
		// Calculate
		function Calulate_peptide(){
			let amount_needed = $('.amount-needed-btn.active').text();
			let peptide = $('.peptide-btn.active').text();
			let bacteria_water = $('.bacteria-water-btn.active').text();
			if(amount_needed == 'Other'){
			  	amount_needed = $('.amount-needed-form input[type=text]').val();
			}else{
				amount_needed = amount_needed.replace('mg','');
			}
			if(peptide == 'Other'){
			  	peptide = $('.peptide-form input[type=text]').val();
			}else{
				peptide = peptide.replace('mg','');
			}
			if(bacteria_water == 'Other'){
			  	bacteria_water = $('.bacteria-water-form input[type=text]').val();
			}else{
				bacteria_water = bacteria_water.replace('mL','');
			}
			amount_needed = parseFloat(amount_needed);
            bacteria_water = parseFloat(bacteria_water);
 
			let total = (amount_needed / (peptide / bacteria_water)) * 100;
			$('.result-amount-needed').text(amount_needed.toFixed(1) + 'mg');
$('.total-result').text(total.toFixed(0) + ' units');
$('.result-bacteria-water').text(bacteria_water.toFixed(1) + 'mg/ml');
 
			console.log('Amount Needed : '+ amount_needed);
			console.log('Peptide : '+ peptide);
			console.log('Bacteria water : '+ bacteria_water);
			console.log('Total : '+ total);
			updateRuler(total);
		}
		Calulate_peptide();
    });
</script>

			<script type="text/rocketlazyloadscript">
let ruler, pointer, highlight;
let height = 650, max = 100;

function createRuler(containerId, h = 650, step = 2, maxVal = 100, pointerAt = 20) {
  height = h;
  max = maxVal;
  const container = document.getElementById(containerId);

  ruler = document.createElement("div");
  ruler.className = "ruler";
  ruler.style.height = height + "px";

  highlight = document.createElement("div");
  highlight.className = "highlight";
  ruler.appendChild(highlight);

  for (let i = 0; i <= max; i += step) {
    const y = (height / max) * i;
    const tick = document.createElement("div");
    tick.className = "tick " + (i % 10 === 0 ? "large" : i % 5 === 0 ? "medium" : "small");
    tick.style.top = y + "px";
    ruler.appendChild(tick);

    if (i % 10 === 0) {
      const label = document.createElement("div");
      label.className = "label";
      label.style.top = (y + 7) + "px";
      label.innerText = i;
      ruler.appendChild(label);
    }
  }

  pointer = document.createElement("div");
  pointer.className = "pointer";
  container.appendChild(pointer);
  container.appendChild(ruler);

  updateRuler(pointerAt);
}

function updateRuler(unit) {
  if (!pointer || !highlight) return;
  const y = (height / max) * unit;
  let pointer_height = (height / max) * unit - 5;
  if (unit > 95) pointer_height = (height / max) * 96;
  pointer.style.top = pointer_height - 10 + "px";
  highlight.style.height = y + "px";
}

createRuler("rulerContainer", 650, 2, 100, 20);
